# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-18)

## Corpus Check
- 3914 files · ~9,970,970 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 86 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 30, .css 16, .entitlements 8)

## Summary
- 57294 nodes · 161420 edges · 1226 communities (1126 shown, 100 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5670 edges (avg confidence: 0.88)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `31b51e15`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
